# Avalonia parity ledger

The single source of truth for "done" in the WPF -> Avalonia 12 port. One row per WPF window,
view, dialog, control, theme dictionary, shell partial and platform feature. The WPF head
(`ConditioningControlPanel/`) is the reference: a row is done when the Avalonia counterpart
matches it or is better, and is at least as fast.

Status, lowest to highest:
- `missing`: no Avalonia counterpart.
- `stub`: a counterpart exists but key behaviour is a placeholder, unwired or unavailable.
- `wired`: the counterpart implements the WPF behaviour as far as the source shows, and something in the
  Avalonia head opens or hosts it. Not yet proven. A surface nothing opens is `stub`, and so is one
  missing a WPF animation or motion effect.
- `verified`: proven on Linux by a headless test or render proof AND a Keincheck run on the live app
  (navigate, click, read state, screenshot), compared against the WPF reference. Evidence linked.
- `improved`: verified, and deliberately better than WPF; the reason is in avalonia-decisions.md.
- `blocked`: cannot progress yet; the blocker is in the notes. Revisit later.

`--render-all` (RenderProof) only proves a view draws; it never makes a row `verified` on its own.
Initial statuses were read from source on 2026-09-28 (stack tip #1765) and are not yet verified.

Starting totals (2026-09-28): 362 rows: 59 missing, 247 stub, 56 wired, 0 verified.

Notes carry what is stubbed or missing, platform-specific parts, existing evidence and, where it
matters, a performance note (startup, tab switch, list scroll, overlays, media).

Flash images now open overlays through this (feat-flash; `X11Overlay.SetOpacity` added: compositor-side alpha, because re-rendering the overlays each fade frame on GLX/XWayland stalled the UI dispatcher ~1.7 s per burst). Windows (win32-overlay-shim): the same entry points route an "HWND" handle to `CCP.Avalonia/Platform/Win32Overlay.cs` (WS_EX_LAYERED|TOOLWINDOW + WS_EX_TRANSPARENT|NOACTIVATE toggled together, kept through Avalonia's style rebuilds via `Win32Properties.AddWindowStylesCallback`, HWND_TOPMOST, LWA_ALPHA opacity; WPF bits from `OverlayService.cs:1415`, `FlashService.cs:4614`/`:4838`); dispatch covered by `Tests/CCP.Avalonia.Tests/OverlayBackendDispatchTests.cs`; compiled for win-x64 only, never run here - verified only by the windows CI `--overlay-check` step (user32 read-back, SKIP without a desktop session). RestackAbove has no Win32 half. Risk: `SetOverrideRedirect` sizes by `DesktopScaling`, which on mixed-DPI Windows comes from the monitor the window was created on, so an overlay on a different-DPI screen may be mis-sized (the CI check has one screen). | shell-single-instance | `ConditioningControlPanel/App.xaml.cs` | `CCP.Avalonia/Program.cs`; `CCP.Avalonia/App.axaml.cs` | stub | Avalonia lifetime starts normally; no mutex, process forwarding, acknowledgment gate or zombie takeover. |
| shell-single-instance | `ConditioningControlPanel/App.xaml.cs` | `CCP.Avalonia/Program.cs`; `CCP.Avalonia/App.axaml.cs` | wired | `CCP.Avalonia/Platform/SingleInstance.cs`, claimed in `Program.cs` before the desktop lifetime: WPF's mutex name `ConditioningControlPanel_SingleInstance_Mutex` (installer AppMutex); a second launch asks the primary to show (ShowFromTray on its UI thread), waits up to WPF's 10 s for the ack and exits 0; no listener = WPF's 8 s mid-update wait, then exit; listener without ack = 3 s wait then run. Show/ack go over a named pipe (Unix socket on Linux) because WPF's named EventWaitHandles do not exist on Unix, so an Avalonia second launch cannot wake a running WPF build. A CCP_USERDATA_DIR sandbox suffixes the names so kc/tests never touch the user's app. `Tests/CCP.Avalonia.Tests/SingleInstanceTests.cs` (fail-proven: no show -> count 0; no ack -> second not null). Live: published linux-x64 build, second launch exit 0 in 0 s with first still up (~/ccp-port/evidence/avalonia-port/publish-profiles/shell-single-instance-live.txt). Not verified: no Keincheck run (Release publish). Launcher handoff (launcher-boot): the request carries the Core `LauncherHandoff` payload (length byte + UTF-8; 0 = bare relaunch) in place of WPF's handoff file, routed by `LauncherWindow.RouteHandoff`; `SingleInstanceTests.SecondLaunchHandsItsSurfaceToThePrimary` fail-proven; live ~/ccp-port/evidence/avalonia-port-launcher-boot/win-launcher-handoff.txt. On Unix .NET's named mutex is session-scoped, so a second launch from another login session (e.g. `setsid`) does not find the primary. Not ported: stale-instance kill (KillStaleInstances), "Open with CCP" file handoff, the startup-phase early ack. |

One frame clock steps it all, only while shown and not minimised and only while something moves; motion Off/Reduced/Performance gate it as WPF does. `LauncherFxTests` (10, stepped clock incl. real-pointer hover and the hover throttle on an injected TimeProvider; each fail-proven, red output ~/ccp-port/evidence/launcher-fx/fail-proofs.txt); spirals park on Deactivated; off-screen Play (game boot, handoff) is silent; a fade guard timer forces the hide; a close drops a pending exit. Every control is live through `CCP.Avalonia/Views/Controls/AppSettings/AudioSettingsBinder.cs` (WPF MainWindow.UiUpdates.cs:1022-1176): master/video/duck sliders and both toggles write settings (master also pushes `LayeredAudio.SetMasterVolumeLive` and a playing mandatory video's volume, ducking-off calls `LibVlcAudio.ForceUnduck`); the output picker lists PulseAudio/PipeWire sinks after "System default" (`LibVlcAudio.EnumerateOutputDevices`, restores by id then name, ↻ re-enumerates); Test Audio shows WPF's "Audio Diagnostics" text and plays chime1 at 50%; the audio-sync pair shows only while `Haptics.AudioSync.Enabled` and writes delay/power (MainWindow.Haptics.cs:1049-1074). | feat-bouncing-text | `ConditioningControlPanel/Features/BouncingTextFeatureControl.xaml`, `ConditioningControlPanel/Features/BouncingTextFeatureControl.xaml.cs` | `CCP.Avalonia/Views/Features/BouncingTextFeatureControl.axaml`, `CCP.Avalonia/Views/Features/BouncingTextFeatureControl.axaml.cs`, `CCP.Avalonia/Views/Overlays/BouncingTextOverlay.cs` | wired | Settings round-trip; the overlay now exists: `CoreBouncingText` seeded in App -> `BouncingTextOverlay` (WPF `Services/Subliminal/BouncingTextService.cs` + `BouncingTextWindow`): one full-screen override-redirect click-through window per targeted screen (GlobalTargetMonitor/DualMonitorEnabled), motion/colours/effects/XP from Core `BouncingTextEngine`, bounds = union of targeted screens / primary scaling and per-window position = WPF UpdatePosition formula, loop per rendered frame (`RequestAnimationFrame`, WPF `CompositionTarget.Rendering`; dt clamp 0.1 s, frozen during DisplayChangeCoordinator), bold BouncingTextFont (Segoe UI fallback), black drop shadow blur 10 depth 3, or Outlined (stroke max(2, size/22), round join, pad stroke+6), scale+rotate transforms, corner-burst ring (240 DIP, 0.1->1.3 ease-out, 550 ms), live Refresh/Restart. Arming: engine Start (the card toggle applies live only while running, as WPF). Tests `Tests/CCP.Avalonia.Tests/TextOverlayTests.cs` (bounds/local placement vs WPF). Live (Keincheck, 3 screens): 3 override-redirect depth-32 windows with 0 input rects at screen bounds, window contents read back from X show the shadowed logo moving ~122 DIP/s at Speed 5, Outlined+Second+Spin restart works, Off -> 0 windows; frame pacing (Debug, 10 s windows): repaint limited to screens near the logo (`IsNear`) took the late-frame share (>2x median) from 0-41.5% (median ~11%) to 0.3-8.9% at 145-155 frames/s, max gap 16-47 ms (startup); gaps were bimodal 144/60 Hz, never below WPF's once-per-refresh rate; process CPU is dominated by the shell's own state (idle 2-23%), feature adds ~0-6%; closing the shell stops every overlay and the process exits (negative control without the hook: process stays alive) (~/ccp-port/evidence/avalonia-port/overlay-text-families/). Pauses (opacity 0, loop stopped) while a mandatory video plays unless Show Over Videos, resumes after (video-attention; live log in ~/ccp-port/evidence/avalonia-port/video-attention/feat-video-duck-and-bouncing-pause.txt). Bounce haptic via CoreHaptics (haptics-core, WPF BouncingTextService.cs:372). Corner hit -> `App.Achievements.TrackCornerHit` (Core `AchievementEngine.TrackCornerHit`, WPF BouncingTextService.cs:355, unlocks corner_hit once) and the 0.5 s topmost re-assert (`X11Overlay.Raise`: XRaiseWindow on X11, HWND_TOPMOST on Win32; WPF ReassertTopmost) run in `BouncingTextOverlay.Advance`; `TextOverlayTests.BouncingText_CornerHitUnlocksTheAchievementAndTheWindowsReassertTopmostEveryHalfSecond` (stepped dt, fail-proven for each). The re-assert accumulates unclamped real dt (WPF "every ~0.5s of real time"): measured re-raise latency 0.40-0.70 s at 1 fps on a locked session (was ~3.5 s with clamped dt), ~/ccp-port/evidence/avalonia-port/rows-floating-text/feat-bouncing-text-topmost-reassert.txt (xstack.py). Spectacle screenshots do not show the overlays (read back from X instead). Main sync #2: bounce walls inset by the drawn overhang (Core `BounceOverhang`, WPF 5609da6fd) in `BouncingTextEngine.Step`; `MainSync2CompatTests.BounceWallsSitInsideTheScreenByTheDrawnOverhang`. Not this row (no consumer on this head): the OnBounce/OnCornerHit bark triggers (folded into `feat-companion-barks`) and the awareness-OCR exclusion rects (GetActiveTextScreenRects, App.xaml.cs:1574). |
| feat-bubble-count | `ConditioningControlPanel/Features/BubbleCountFeatureControl.xaml`, `ConditioningControlPanel/Features/BubbleCountFeatureControl.xaml.cs` | `CCP.Avalonia/Views/Features/BubbleCountFeatureControl.axaml`, `CCP.Avalonia/Views/Features/BubbleCountFeatureControl.axaml.cs` | stub | Studio > Bubble Count. MarkWhisperAudio n/a until a bark engine runs on this head (CoreBark unseeded). Perf finding (session-runner-wire: cards 1.1-3.1 s with flash + bouncing text) FIXED (overlay-first-frame-perf): root cause was `_NET_WM_WINDOW_OPACITY` 0, at which every overlay maps and ends its fade - KWin sends an opacity-0 XWayland window no frame callbacks, so the vsync'd swap waits for XWayland's 1 s fallback and stalls every overlay's frames and the UI thread 1-2 s. `X11Overlay.OpacityCardinal` floors the value at 1/(2^32-1) (still invisible), and a backstop closes each card at hold + 200 ms after Show() however late its first frame. Live 150 s flash + subliminal + bouncing text: cards p95 0.29 s max 0.29 s (before p95 3.06 s), card first frame median 11 ms (before 1382 ms), bouncing-text max frame gap 92 ms (before 2097 ms), flash lives 6.38-6.45 s (before 6.37-8.82 s). Tests `TextOverlayTests.SubliminalCard_ClosesOnTime_EvenWithoutAFirstFrame` + `OverlayOpacity_IsNeverExactlyZero`, both fail-proven. Evidence ~/ccp-port/evidence/avalonia-port/overlay-first-frame-perf/. |

Tests `Tests/CCP.Core.Tests/AiCommandGateTests.cs` (fail-proven: switch, Lab, per-effect, cap removed; nested re-gate, follow-up cap and CancelAll removed), `Tests/CCP.Avalonia.Tests/CompanionEffectsTests.cs` (fail-proven: audio-only ignored, overlay refusal removed, lock card always fired), `MandatoryVideoSchedulerTests.TriggerWithAPathPlaysThatClipUnstrict` (fail-proven: path ignored). Not live-checked (effects need a Lab account). Plan for the rest: `~/ccp-port/briefs/companion-brain-plan.md`. |
| feat-companion-brain | `ConditioningControlPanel/Services/Companion/Brain/{CompanionBrain,PromptAssembler,MemoryStore,CompanionSessionStore,ChatSession,CompanionTurn,ConversationRecall,ExplicitMemory,CompanionMemoryMaintenance}.cs`, `Services/Companion/{ConversationDelivery,ConversationRelationship}.cs` | Moved to `CCP.Core/Services/Companion/` (git mv; App.* -> CoreSettings/CoreAccount/CoreMods/CoreModerationLog/LockdownService.Current; head seams CompanionBrain.{UserMessageSent,CommandExecutor,EffectScheduler,ClearLegacyLocalHistoryHook,MemoryRecalled,ActivitiesProvider}, MemoryStore.{SignalMirrorFactory,WireDeferredSignalsHook}, PromptAssembler.{NoticeSurface,PossessionState}, all seeded by WPF App.xaml.cs); `App.Brain` in `CCP.Avalonia/App.axaml.cs`, used by `AvatarTubeWindow.SendChatAsync` | stub | Tube chat sends through the brain while UseCompanionBrain is on (default) and the stateless call when off or the brain failed to build, as WPF ChatInput.cs:772. Turn log and memory in CorePaths.UserData/companion/{session,memory}.json, same format as WPF; moderation unchanged (AiService in+out, MemoryStore storage gate via CoreModerationLog.Guard). Test `Tests/CCP.Avalonia.Tests/TubeChatBrainTests.cs` (loopback fake proxy, companion protocol 2; fail-proven: route forced off, kill switch ignored). Slice 2 (companion-brain-tube): thinking bubble + double bounce around the reply; `CompanionAskService`/`AskCard`/`AskCatalog`/`AskPlanner` git mv'd to `CCP.Core/Services/Companion/Asks/` (App.* -> seams BrainProvider/ShowCardSurface/SaySurface/BusyProvider/SessionOptions/StartSession/OpenLink, WPF `App.SeedAskSeams`), Avalonia seeds them plus `ConversationDelivery.AskCardsShown` (WPF: always; Avalonia: only when a card can be built for that input and nothing is busy) and `PromptAssembler.NoticeSurface` (oversize-prompt toast via `App.Notifications`) in `App.SeedCompanionTubeSeams`; test `Tests/CCP.Avalonia.Tests/TubeChatParityTests.cs` (fail-proven: no thinking, no bounce, no offer, chip click dead, AskCardsShown unseeded, thinking not stopped). Stub, per the ledger rule: bark echo (no bark engine on this head), the UserMessageSent achievement emit, AI effect commands, activity/session offers (no launcher: Session/Game/Quests cards never build; a Watch link opens externally, WPF's fallback), Busy = session, video, lock card, bubble count, pop quiz (no grace pause) and the possession rung. Slice 3 (companion-brain-signals): `MemorySignalWriter` git mv'd to `CCP.Core/Services/Companion/Brain/` (settings mirror over CoreSettings; App.* sources -> seams `SourcesHook`/`DeferredSourcesHook`, WPF `App.WireMemorySignalSources` + mantra hook, bodies verbatim); Avalonia `App.SeedMemorySignals` seeds `SignalMirrorFactory`: profile (level/streak/sessions/archetype/username), `ProgressionBank.LevelUp`, favourite features flash/subliminal/bubbles (`App.FeatureUsed`) and video (Core `MandatoryVideoScheduler.VideoStarted`), per-mod chat turns (`App.UserMessageSent`, raised by the brain and the tube's non-brain path, as WPF ChatInput.cs:788; counted only with companion v2 off, as WPF). Tests `Tests/CCP.Avalonia.Tests/MemorySignalSeedTests.cs`, `Tests/CCP.Core.Tests/MandatoryVideoSchedulerTests.cs` (fail-proven x3: FeatureUsed wire removed, writer unsubscribe removed, VideoStarted not raised). Live: memory.json profile written at startup in a sandbox (`~/ccp-port/evidence/avalonia-port/companion-brain-signals/feat-companion-brain-memory-profile-live.json`); feature/chat signals not triggered live. Mantra favourite: `App.Mantra.MantraCompleted` wired in `App.SeedMemorySignals` (WPF DeferredSourcesHook; test `MantraWindowSessionTests`, fail-proven). Still missing: brain drain/mind wipe favourites (no such services on this head), bark echo (`AttachBarkSource`: no Core bark engine hosted). Slice 5 (companion-awareness-brain): awareness leg = WPF legacy route only (see shell-awareness); `AwarenessV2Routing`/`AwarenessSpeech`/`ReactionArbiter` stay in the WPF head (dry-run: drags AwarenessReactionService, AwarenessPromptBuilder, WorthinessScorer -> ActivityLedger and ~25 App.* sites, and they consume only v2 observer frames, which this head lacks; decision 2026-10-01); bark echo still missing. Plan for the rest: `~/ccp-port/briefs/companion-brain-plan.md`. |
